### 8.3 基准测试脚本（Python）

#### 8.3.1 音乐分析基准测试

```python
# benchmarks/music_analysis_benchmark.py
"""音乐分析基准测试"""

import time
import json
import os
import numpy as np
import librosa
from typing import Dict, List
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class BenchmarkResult:
    """基准测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    audio_file: str
    duration: float
    sample_rate: int
    total_time: float
    stages: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    metrics: Dict[str, any] = field(default_factory=dict)

class MusicAnalysisBenchmark:
    """音乐分析基准测试套件"""
    
    def __init__(self, audio_dir: str, output_dir: str = "./benchmark_results"):
        self.audio_dir = audio_dir
        self.output_dir = output_dir
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self.results: List[BenchmarkResult] = []
        self.sample_rate = 44100
    
    def run_all(self):
        """运行所有基准测试"""
        audio_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(self.audio_dir) if f.endswith('.mp3')])
        
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  音乐分析基准测试")
        print("=" * 60)
        print(f"测试音频数量: {len(audio_files)}")
        print(f"采样率: {self.sample_rate} Hz")
        print()
        
        for audio_file in audio_files:
            result = self.run_single(audio_file)
            self.results.append(result)
            self._print_result(result)
        
        self._generate_summary()
        self._save_results()
    
    def run_single(self, audio_file: str) -> BenchmarkResult:
        """运行单个音频的基准测试"""
        audio_path = os.path.join(self.audio_dir, audio_file)
        
        total_start = time.time()
        stages = {}
        
        # 阶段1: 音频加载
        t0 = time.time()
        y, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=self.sample_rate, mono=True)
        duration = len(y) / sr
        stages["load_audio"] = time.time() - t0
        
        # 阶段2: BPM检测
        t0 = time.time()
        tempo, beat_frames = librosa.beat.beat_track(y=y, sr=sr)
        beat_times = librosa.frames_to_time(beat_frames, sr=sr)
        stages["bpm_detection"] = time.time() - t0
        
        # 阶段3: 频段能量分解
        t0 = time.time()
        S = np.abs(librosa.stft(y, n_fft=2048, hop_length=512))
        freqs = librosa.fft_frequencies(sr=sr, n_fft=2048)
        
        bands = {
            "sub_bass": (20, 60),
            "bass": (60, 250),
            "low_mid": (250, 500),
            "mid": (500, 2000),
            "high_mid": (2000, 4000),
            "high": (4000, 20000)
        }
        
        band_energy = {}
        for band_name, (f_low, f_high) in bands.items():
            mask = (freqs >= f_low) & (freqs <= f_high)
            energy = np.mean(S[mask, :], axis=0) if np.any(mask) else np.zeros(S.shape[1])
            band_energy[band_name] = float(np.mean(energy))
        stages["band_energy"] = time.time() - t0
        
        # 阶段4: 色度特征
        t0 = time.time()
        chroma = librosa.feature.chroma_stft(y=y, sr=sr)
        stages["chroma"] = time.time() - t0
        
        # 阶段5: 频谱特征
        t0 = time.time()
        spectral_centroid = librosa.feature.spectral_centroid(y=y, sr=sr)
        spectral_bandwidth = librosa.feature.spectral_bandwidth(y=y, sr=sr)
        spectral_rolloff = librosa.feature.spectral_rolloff(y=y, sr=sr)
        stages["spectral_features"] = time.time() - t0
        
        # 阶段6: 节奏特征
        t0 = time.time()
        onset_env = librosa.onset.onset_strength(y=y, sr=sr)
        tempo_2, beat_frames_2 = librosa.beat.beat_track(onset_envelope=onset_env, sr=sr)
        stages["rhythm_features"] = time.time() - t0
        
        total_time = time.time() - total_start
        
        result = BenchmarkResult(
            test_name="music_analysis",
            audio_file=audio_file,
            duration=duration,
            sample_rate=sr,
            total_time=total_time,
            stages=stages,
            metrics={
                "bpm": float(tempo),
                "beat_count": len(beat_frames),
                "band_energy": band_energy,
                "spectral_centroid_mean": float(np.mean(spectral_centroid)),
                "realtime_factor": total_time / duration
            }
        )
        
        return result
    
    def _print_result(self, result: BenchmarkResult):
        """打印单个结果"""
        print(f"--- {result.audio_file} ---")
        print(f"  时长: {result.duration:.2f}s")
        print(f"  总耗时: {result.total_time:.3f}s")
        print(f"  实时因子: {result.metrics['realtime_factor']:.3f}x")
        print(f"  BPM: {result.metrics['bpm']:.1f}")
        print(f"  各阶段耗时:")
        for stage, t in sorted(result.stages.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True):
            pct = t / result.total_time * 100
            bar = "█" * int(pct / 5) + "░" * (20 - int(pct / 5))
            print(f"    {stage:20s} {bar} {t:.3f}s ({pct:.1f}%)")
        print()
    
    def _generate_summary(self):
        """生成汇总报告"""
        if not self.results:
            return
        
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  汇总报告")
        print("=" * 60)
        
        total_durations = sum(r.duration for r in self.results)
        total_times = sum(r.total_time for r in self.results)
        avg_rtf = total_times / total_durations
        
        print(f"总音频数: {len(self.results)}")
        print(f"总时长: {total_durations:.1f}s ({total_durations/60:.1f}min)")
        print(f"总处理时间: {total_times:.1f}s ({total_times/60:.1f}min)")
        print(f"平均实时因子: {avg_rtf:.3f}x")
        print()
        
        # 各阶段平均耗时
        all_stages = set()
        for r in self.results:
            all_stages.update(r.stages.keys())
        
        print("各阶段平均耗时:")
        for stage in sorted(all_stages):
            times = [r.stages[stage] for r in self.results if stage in r.stages]
            avg = np.mean(times)
            print(f"  {stage}: {avg:.3f}s")
        
        print()
        
        # BPM分布
        bpm_values = [r.metrics.get("bpm", 0) for r in self.results]
        print(f"BPM范围: {min(bpm_values):.1f} - {max(bpm_values):.1f}")
        print(f"BPM均值: {np.mean(bpm_values):.1f}")
        print()
    
    def _save_results(self):
        """保存结果到文件"""
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        filename = f"music_benchmark_{timestamp}.json"
        filepath = os.path.join(self.output_dir, filename)
        
        results_data = [asdict(r) for r in self.results]
        
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(results_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print(f"结果已保存到: {filepath}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    benchmark = MusicAnalysisBenchmark("./test_dataset/audio")
    benchmark.run_all()
```

#### 8.3.2 视频分析基准测试

```python
# benchmarks/video_analysis_benchmark.py
"""视频分析基准测试"""

import time
import json
import os
import cv2
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class VideoBenchmarkResult:
    """视频分析基准测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    video_file: str
    duration: float
    resolution: tuple
    fps: float
    total_frames: int
    total_time: float
    frames_processed: int = 0
    stages: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    metrics: Dict[str, any] = field(default_factory=dict)

class VideoAnalysisBenchmark:
    """视频分析基准测试套件"""
    
    def __init__(self, video_dir: str, output_dir: str = "./benchmark_results",
                 sample_interval: int = 5):
        self.video_dir = video_dir
        self.output_dir = output_dir
        self.sample_interval = sample_interval
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self.results: List[VideoBenchmarkResult] = []
    
    def run_all(self):
        """运行所有基准测试"""
        video_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(self.video_dir) if f.endswith('.mp4')])
        
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  视频分析基准测试")
        print("=" * 60)
        print(f"测试视频数量: {len(video_files)}")
        print(f"采样间隔: 每{self.sample_interval}帧处理1帧")
        print()
        
        for video_file in video_files:
            result = self.run_single(video_file)
            self.results.append(result)
            self._print_result(result)
        
        self._generate_summary()
        self._save_results()
    
    def run_single(self, video_file: str) -> VideoBenchmarkResult:
        """运行单个视频的基准测试"""
        video_path = os.path.join(self.video_dir, video_file)
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
        
        width = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
        height = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
        total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
        duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0
        
        total_start = time.time()
        stages = {}
        
        frame_count = 0
        processed_frames = 0
        
        hist_sum = np.zeros(256)
        brightness_values = []
        motion_values = []
        prev_gray = None
        
        # 阶段: 逐帧处理
        t0 = time.time()
        
        while True:
            ret, frame = cap.read()
            if not ret:
                break
            
            frame_count += 1
            
            if frame_count % self.sample_interval != 0:
                continue
            
            processed_frames += 1
            
            # 颜色直方图
            gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
            hist = cv2.calcHist([gray], [0], None, [256], [0, 256]).flatten()
            hist_sum += hist
            
            # 亮度
            brightness = np.mean(gray)
            brightness_values.append(brightness)
            
            # 运动检测 (光流简化版)
            if prev_gray is not None:
                flow = cv2.calcOpticalFlowFarneback(
                    prev_gray, gray, None,
                    0.5, 3, 15, 3, 5, 1.2, 0
                )
                motion_mag = np.mean(np.sqrt(flow[..., 0]**2 + flow[..., 1]**2))
                motion_values.append(motion_mag)
            
            prev_gray = gray.copy()
        
        stages["frame_processing"] = time.time() - t0
        
        cap.release()
        
        total_time = time.time() - total_start
        
        result = VideoBenchmarkResult(
            test_name="video_analysis",
            video_file=video_file,
            duration=duration,
            resolution=(width, height),
            fps=fps,
            total_frames=total_frames,
            total_time=total_time,
            frames_processed=processed_frames,
            stages=stages,
            metrics={
                "fps_processing": processed_frames / stages.get("frame_processing", 1),
                "realtime_factor": total_time / duration if duration > 0 else 0,
                "avg_brightness": float(np.mean(brightness_values)) if brightness_values else 0,
                "avg_motion": float(np.mean(motion_values)) if motion_values else 0,
                "brightness_std": float(np.std(brightness_values)) if brightness_values else 0,
                "motion_std": float(np.std(motion_values)) if motion_values else 0,
            }
        )
        
        return result
    
    def _print_result(self, result: VideoBenchmarkResult):
        """打印单个结果"""
        print(f"--- {result.video_file} ---")
        print(f"  分辨率: {result.resolution[0]}x{result.resolution[1]}")
        print(f"  帧率: {result.fps:.1f} fps")
        print(f"  总帧数: {result.total_frames}")
        print(f"  时长: {result.duration:.2f}s")
        print(f"  处理帧数: {result.frames_processed}")
        print(f"  总耗时: {result.total_time:.3f}s")
        print(f"  处理速度: {result.metrics['fps_processing']:.1f} frames/s")
        print(f"  实时因子: {result.metrics['realtime_factor']:.3f}x")
        print()
    
    def _generate_summary(self):
        """生成汇总报告"""
        if not self.results:
            return
        
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  汇总报告")
        print("=" * 60)
        
        total_durations = sum(r.duration for r in self.results)
        total_times = sum(r.total_time for r in self.results)
        avg_rtf = total_times / total_durations if total_durations > 0 else 0
        avg_fps = np.mean([r.metrics['fps_processing'] for r in self.results])
        
        print(f"总视频数: {len(self.results)}")
        print(f"总时长: {total_durations:.1f}s ({total_durations/60:.1f}min)")
        print(f"总处理时间: {total_times:.1f}s ({total_times/60:.1f}min)")
        print(f"平均实时因子: {avg_rtf:.3f}x")
        print(f"平均处理速度: {avg_fps:.1f} frames/s")
        print()
    
    def _save_results(self):
        """保存结果到文件"""
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        filename = f"video_benchmark_{timestamp}.json"
        filepath = os.path.join(self.output_dir, filename)
        
        results_data = [asdict(r) for r in self.results]
        
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(results_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print(f"结果已保存到: {filepath}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    benchmark = VideoAnalysisBenchmark("./test_dataset/video")
    benchmark.run_all()
```

#### 8.3.3 匹配算法基准测试

```python
# benchmarks/matching_benchmark.py
"""匹配算法基准测试"""

import time
import json
import os
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from datetime import datetime
from scipy.optimize import linear_sum_assignment

@dataclass
class MatchingBenchmarkResult:
    """匹配算法基准测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    algorithm: str
    num_beats: int
    num_clips: int
    num_matches: int
    total_time: float
    metrics: Dict[str, any] = field(default_factory=dict)

class MatchingBenchmark:
    """匹配算法基准测试套件"""
    
    def __init__(self, output_dir: str = "./benchmark_results"):
        self.output_dir = output_dir
        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
        self.results: List[MatchingBenchmarkResult] = []
    
    def generate_test_data(self, num_beats: int, num_clips: int) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """生成测试数据"""
        np.random.seed(42)
        
        beat_features = np.random.rand(num_beats, 10)
        clip_features = np.random.rand(num_clips, 10)
        
        return beat_features, clip_features
    
    def compute_similarity_matrix(self, beat_features: np.ndarray, 
                                   clip_features: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """计算相似度矩阵"""
        n_beats = beat_features.shape[0]
        n_clips = clip_features.shape[0]
        
        similarity = np.zeros((n_beats, n_clips))
        for i in range(n_beats):
            for j in range(n_clips):
                similarity[i, j] = 1 - np.linalg.norm(beat_features[i] - clip_features[j])
        
        return similarity
    
    def run_dp_matching(self, similarity: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[int, int]], float]:
        """动态规划匹配"""
        n, m = similarity.shape
        
        dp = np.zeros((n + 1, m + 1))
        path = np.zeros((n + 1, m + 1), dtype=int)
        
        for i in range(1, n + 1):
            for j in range(1, m + 1):
                choices = [
                    dp[i-1, j],
                    dp[i, j-1],
                    dp[i-1, j-1] + similarity[i-1, j-1]
                ]
                best = np.argmax(choices)
                dp[i, j] = choices[best]
                path[i, j] = best
        
        matches = []
        i, j = n, m
        while i > 0 and j > 0:
            if path[i, j] == 2:
                matches.append((i-1, j-1))
                i -= 1
                j -= 1
            elif path[i, j] == 0:
                i -= 1
            else:
                j -= 1
        
        matches.reverse()
        return matches, dp[n, m]
    
    def run_hungarian_matching(self, similarity: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[int, int]], float]:
        """匈牙利算法匹配"""
        n, m = similarity.shape
        size = max(n, m)
        
        cost_matrix = np.zeros((size, size))
        cost_matrix[:n, :m] = -similarity
        
        row_ind, col_ind = linear_sum_assignment(cost_matrix)
        
        matches = []
        total_score = 0
        for r, c in zip(row_ind, col_ind):
            if r < n and c < m:
                matches.append((r, c))
                total_score += similarity[r, c]
        
        return matches, total_score
    
    def run_greedy_matching(self, similarity: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[int, int]], float]:
        """贪心匹配"""
        n, m = similarity.shape
        
        used_clips = set()
        matches = []
        total_score = 0
        
        for i in range(n):
            best_j = -1
            best_score = -np.inf
            for j in range(m):
                if j not in used_clips and similarity[i, j] > best_score:
                    best_score = similarity[i, j]
                    best_j = j
            
            if best_j >= 0:
                matches.append((i, best_j))
                used_clips.add(best_j)
                total_score += best_score
        
        return matches, total_score
    
    def run_all(self):
        """运行所有基准测试"""
        test_cases = [
            (50, 30, "小规模"),
            (100, 50, "中规模"),
            (200, 100, "大规模"),
            (500, 200, "超大规模"),
        ]
        
        algorithms = [
            ("dp", "动态规划", self.run_dp_matching),
            ("hungarian", "匈牙利算法", self.run_hungarian_matching),
            ("greedy", "贪心算法", self.run_greedy_matching),
        ]
        
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  匹配算法基准测试")
        print("=" * 60)
        print()
        
        for num_beats, num_clips, scale_name in test_cases:
            print(f"--- {scale_name}: {num_beats} beats × {num_clips} clips ---")
            
            beat_features, clip_features = self.generate_test_data(num_beats, num_clips)
            similarity = self.compute_similarity_matrix(beat_features, clip_features)
            
            for algo_id, algo_name, algo_func in algorithms:
                t0 = time.time()
                matches, total_score = algo_func(similarity)
                elapsed = time.time() - t0
                
                result = MatchingBenchmarkResult(
                    test_name=f"matching_{scale_name}",
                    algorithm=algo_id,
                    num_beats=num_beats,
                    num_clips=num_clips,
                    num_matches=len(matches),
                    total_time=elapsed,
                    metrics={
                        "total_score": float(total_score),
                        "avg_score_per_match": float(total_score / len(matches)) if matches else 0,
                        "match_ratio": len(matches) / min(num_beats, num_clips)
                    }
                )
                self.results.append(result)
                
                print(f"  {algo_name:10s}: {elapsed:.4f}s, "
                      f"匹配数={len(matches)}, "
                      f"总分={total_score:.2f}")
            
            print()
        
        self._save_results()
    
    def _save_results(self):
        """保存结果到文件"""
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        filename = f"matching_benchmark_{timestamp}.json"
        filepath = os.path.join(self.output_dir, filename)
        
        results_data = [asdict(r) for r in self.results]
        
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(results_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        
        print(f"结果已保存到: {filepath}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    benchmark = MatchingBenchmark()
    benchmark.run_all()
```

---

### 8.4 性能基准参考

#### 8.4.1 音乐分析性能基准

| 硬件配置 | 处理速度 (实时因子) | 单首3分钟歌曲耗时 |
|----------|---------------------|-------------------|
| 入门级 (i5-8代, 8G内存) | 0.5-0.8x | 3:45 - 6:00 |
| 主流级 (i5-12代, 16G内存) | 1.5-2.5x | 1:12 - 2:00 |
| 高性能 (i7-13代, 32G内存) | 3-5x | 0:36 - 1:00 |
| 工作站级 (i9-13代, 64G内存) | 5-8x | 0:22 - 0:36 |

#### 8.4.2 视频分析性能基准

| 硬件配置 | 1080p处理速度 (fps) | 4K处理速度 (fps) | 1分钟视频耗时 |
|----------|---------------------|-------------------|---------------|
| 入门级 (i5-8代, CPU only) | 15-25 | 4-8 | 1:12 - 2:00 |
| 主流级 (i5-12代, CPU only) | 40-60 | 10-15 | 0:30 - 0:45 |
| 高性能 (i7-13代 + GPU) | 100-150 | 30-50 | 0:12 - 0:18 |
| 工作站级 (i9 + RTX 4090) | 200-300 | 60-100 | 0:06 - 0:09 |

#### 8.4.3 匹配算法性能基准

| 算法 | 100×50 | 500×200 | 1000×500 | 时间复杂度 |
|------|--------|---------|----------|-----------|
| 贪心算法 | <1ms | 5ms | 20ms | O(n×m) |
| 匈牙利算法 | 5ms | 100ms | 500ms | O(n³) |
| 动态规划 | 10ms | 200ms | 800ms | O(n×m) |

> [!note] 性能基准说明
> - 以上数据为参考值，实际性能受代码优化程度、数据特征、系统负载等因素影响
> - 建议在自己的开发环境中运行基准测试，建立个人基准线
> - 性能优化应该基于实际测量，而不是猜测

---

### 8.5 质量基准参考

#### 8.5.1 音乐分析质量基准

| 指标 | 入门级 | 基础级 | 进阶级 | 专家级 | 大师级 |
|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|
| BPM检测准确率 | ±5 BPM | ±3 BPM | ±2 BPM | ±1 BPM | ±0.5 BPM |
| 节拍点偏移 | ±50ms | ±30ms | ±20ms | ±10ms | ±5ms |
| 段落分割准确率 | 60% | 75% | 85% | 92% | 95%+ |
| 情绪识别相关度 | 0.5 | 0.6 | 0.7 | 0.8 | 0.85+ |

#### 8.5.2 视频分析质量基准

| 指标 | 入门级 | 基础级 | 进阶级 | 专家级 | 大师级 |
|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|
| 场景检测准确率 | 70% | 80% | 88% | 93% | 96%+ |
| 运动估计误差 | 大 | 中 | 小 | 极小 | 极小+稳定 |
| 色彩分析偏差 | 明显 | 中等 | 较小 | 微小 | 人眼不可察 |
| 构图评分相关度 | 0.4 | 0.55 | 0.7 | 0.8 | 0.85+ |

#### 8.5.3 音画匹配质量基准

| 指标 | 入门级 | 基础级 | 进阶级 | 专家级 | 大师级 |
|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|
| 节拍对齐率 | 50% | 70% | 85% | 92% | 96%+ |
| 风格匹配度 (10分制) | 4分 | 6分 | 7.5分 | 8.5分 | 9分+ |
| 人工A/B测试偏好率 | 30% | 45% | 60% | 75% | 85%+ |
| 情绪传达准确度 | 50% | 65% | 78% | 88% | 92%+ |

> [!tip] 如何测量这些指标
> - **节拍对齐率**：人工标注节拍点，计算系统输出与标注的匹配比例
> - **风格匹配度**：请多名评估者打分，取平均值
> - **A/B测试偏好率**：与人工剪辑进行成对比较，统计偏好比例
> - **情绪传达准确度**：比较预期情绪和观众实际感受的相关度

---

### 8.6 如何跑基准测试

#### 8.6.1 测试前准备

```bash
# 1. 准备测试数据集
# 将测试音乐放到 test_dataset/audio/
# 将测试视频放到 test_dataset/video/

# 2. 安装依赖
pip install librosa opencv-python scipy numpy

# 3. 创建结果目录
mkdir benchmark_results
```

#### 8.6.2 运行测试

```bash
# 运行音乐分析基准
python benchmarks/music_analysis_benchmark.py

# 运行视频分析基准
python benchmarks/video_analysis_benchmark.py

# 运行匹配算法基准
python benchmarks/matching_benchmark.py
```

#### 8.6.3 记录成绩

创建个人基准记录：

```markdown
# 个人基准测试记录

## 测试环境
- 日期: 2026-07-01
- CPU: Intel i7-13700K
- 内存: 32GB DDR5
- GPU: RTX 4070
- 系统: Windows 11

|--------|------|------|
```

> [!important] 基准测试注意事项
> 1. **保持环境一致**：测试时关闭其他占用资源的程序
> 2. **多次测试取平均**：运行3次以上，取平均值以减少随机波动
> 3. **记录硬件配置**：不同硬件的性能差异很大
> 4. **定期重测**：代码修改后重新测试，跟踪性能变化
> 5. **关注趋势**：和自己的历史数据对比，而不是和别人比

---

## 第9章 A/B测试方法论

### 9.1 什么是A/B测试

A/B测试（也叫对照实验、分割测试）是一种科学的对比实验方法。在视频剪辑领域，A/B测试指的是：将两种（或多种）剪辑方案呈现给评估者，通过统计分析判断哪种方案更优。

> [!abstract] A/B测试的核心思想
> - **控制变量**：只改变一个因素，其他保持一致
> - **随机分配**：评估者随机分组，避免偏差
> - **统计检验**：用数学方法判断差异是否显著
> - **数据驱动**：用数据说话，而不是凭感觉

**参考文档**：[[质量保证与测试体系深度研究报告]]、[[用户体验评估方法论]]

---

### 9.2 A/B测试在视频剪辑中的应用

#### 9.2.1 常见测试场景

| 测试类型 | 测试目的 | 变量示例 |
|----------|----------|----------|
| 节拍对齐测试 | 哪种对齐方式更有节奏感 | A: 强拍对齐 / B: 弱拍对齐 |
| 转场效果测试 | 哪种转场更自然 | A: 淡入淡出 / B: 闪白 |
| 效果强度测试 | 效果强度多少最合适 | A: 低强度 / B: 高强度 |
| 剪辑节奏测试 | 剪辑频率多少最舒服 | A: 快节奏 / B: 慢节奏 |
| 色彩风格测试 | 哪种色调更受欢迎 | A: 冷色调 / B: 暖色调 |
| 匹配算法测试 | 哪种算法匹配效果好 | A: 匈牙利 / B: 动态规划 |
| 流派配方测试 | 哪个配方更符合风格 | A: v1配方 / B: v2配方 |

#### 9.2.2 不适合A/B测试的场景

- **探索性创作**：没有明确目标，需要发散思维
- **艺术性极强的判断**：审美差异太大，难以量化
- **样本量不足**：评估者太少，结果不可靠
- **变量太多**：无法控制单一变量

---

### 9.3 A/B测试设计原则

#### 9.3.1 控制变量原则

**一次只测试一个变量。** 如果同时改变多个因素，就无法知道是哪个因素导致了结果差异。

> [!example] 正确 vs 错误
> **正确**：测试转场效果 → 只改变转场类型，其他（音乐、片段、节奏、效果）完全相同
>
> **错误**：同时改变转场效果和效果强度 → 不知道是转场还是强度导致了差异

如果需要测试多个变量，可以使用**多变量测试（MVT）**，但设计和分析会更复杂。

#### 9.3.2 样本量原则

**样本量越大，结果越可靠。** 样本量太小的话，结果可能只是随机波动。

**样本量估算方法：**

```python
# sample_size_calculator.py
"""样本量计算器"""

import math
from scipy import stats

def calculate_sample_size(baseline_rate: float, 
                          minimum_effect: float,
                          alpha: float = 0.05,
                          power: float = 0.8) -> int:
    """
    计算A/B测试所需样本量
    
    参数:
        baseline_rate: 基准转化率/偏好率 (0-1)
        minimum_effect: 最小可检测效应 (绝对值)
        alpha: 显著性水平 (Type I error)
        power: 统计功效 (1 - Type II error)
    
    返回:
        每组需要的样本量
    """
    z_alpha = stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2)
    z_beta = stats.norm.ppf(power)
    
    p1 = baseline_rate
    p2 = baseline_rate + minimum_effect
    
    # 合并比例
    p_pooled = (p1 + p2) / 2
    
    # 样本量公式
    n = (
        (z_alpha * math.sqrt(2 * p_pooled * (1 - p_pooled)) +
         z_beta * math.sqrt(p1 * (1 - p1) + p2 * (1 - p2)))
        ** 2 / (p2 - p1) ** 2
    )
    
    return math.ceil(n)

# 使用示例
if __name__ == "__main__":
    baseline = 0.5  # 基准50%偏好率
    min_effect = 0.15  # 想检测15%的差异
    
    sample_size = calculate_sample_size(baseline, min_effect)
    print(f"基准偏好率: {baseline*100:.0f}%")
    print(f"最小可检测效应: {min_effect*100:.0f}%")
    print(f"每组需要样本量: {sample_size}")
    print(f"总共需要: {sample_size * 2} 个评估")
    print()
    
    # 不同效应大小的样本量需求
    print("不同效应大小的样本量需求:")
    for effect in [0.05, 0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.3]:
        n = calculate_sample_size(0.5, effect)
        print(f"  {effect*100:.0f}% 差异: 每组{n}人 (共{n*2}人)")
```

#### 9.3.3 随机性原则

**评估者应该被随机分配到不同组。** 避免系统性偏差，比如：
- 不要让专家都评估A版本，新手都评估B版本
- 不要按时间顺序，先让所有人看A再看B（会有学习效应）
- 不要让同一个人评估太多（会疲劳）

**随机化方法：**
1. 完全随机：每个人随机看A或B
2. 配对设计：每个人都看A和B，但顺序随机
3. 拉丁方：多种条件平衡顺序效应

#### 9.3.4 双盲原则

**评估者不知道哪个是A哪个是B。** 避免心理暗示影响判断。

实现方法：
- 版本命名中性化（版本1 / 版本2，而不是旧版 / 新版）
- 播放顺序随机化
- 评估者不知道测试目的

---

### 9.4 测试维度设计

#### 9.4.1 核心评估维度

| 维度 | 定义 | 评估要点 |
|------|------|----------|
| 节拍对齐 | 画面切换是否贴合音乐节拍 | 是否踩点、节奏感强弱 |
| 风格匹配 | 视觉风格是否符合音乐气质 | 调性是否一致、氛围是否契合 |
| 节奏感受 | 整体剪辑节奏是否舒适 | 快慢是否得当、有无拖沓或仓促 |
| 情绪传达 | 是否传达了预期的情绪 | 情绪是否准确、感染力强弱 |
| 流畅度 | 整体观看是否流畅自然 | 有无突兀感、转场是否自然 |
| 整体偏好 | 综合来看更喜欢哪个 | 综合打分、二选一 |

#### 9.4.2 Likert 7点量表

Likert量表是最常用的主观评估工具。7点量表比5点更灵敏，比9点更易操作。

```
节拍对齐：
1 = 完全不对齐  2 = 不太对齐  3 = 稍微不对齐  4 = 一般
5 = 稍微对齐    6 = 比较对齐  7 = 非常对齐

风格匹配：
1 = 完全不匹配  2 = 不太匹配  3 = 稍微不匹配  4 = 一般
5 = 稍微匹配    6 = 比较匹配  7 = 非常匹配
```

> [!tip] 量表设计注意事项
> 1. **保持中立点**：必须有中间选项（第4点）
> 2. **对称设计**：正向和负向选项数量对称
> 3. **描述清晰**：每个选项都有文字描述，避免歧义
> 4. **避免诱导**：不要使用带有倾向性的描述

#### 9.4.3 成对比较法

对于整体偏好，最简单有效的方法是**成对比较**：直接问评估者更喜欢哪个。

```
你更喜欢哪个版本？
○ 版本A
○ 版本B
○ 差不多
```

成对比较的优点：
- 简单直观，评估者容易理解
- 信度高，比打分更可靠
- 可以计算偏好率

---

### 9.5 统计显著性检验

#### 9.5.1 为什么需要统计检验

你做了一个A/B测试，10个人里有7个人更喜欢A版本。能说A更好吗？

不一定。可能只是随机波动。想象一下抛硬币，10次里出现7次正面，你能说这枚硬币不均匀吗？

统计检验就是帮我们判断：**观测到的差异是真实存在的，还是只是随机巧合？**

#### 9.5.2 常用统计检验方法

| 数据类型 | 检验方法 | 适用场景 |
|----------|----------|----------|
| 二分类数据（偏好率） | 卡方检验 / Fisher精确检验 | A/B二选一 |
| 连续数据（评分） | t检验 / Mann-Whitney U检验 | Likert量表打分 |
| 多组比较 | 方差分析（ANOVA） | 3个以上版本对比 |
| 配对数据 | 配对t检验 / Wilcoxon检验 | 同一人评估两个版本 |

#### 9.5.3 Python实现

```python
# ab_test_analysis.py
"""A/B测试统计分析工具"""

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Tuple, Optional

@dataclass
class ABTestResult:
    """A/B测试结果"""
    test_type: str
    a_size: int
    b_size: int
    a_mean: float
    b_mean: float
    difference: float
    p_value: float
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]
    significant: bool
    effect_size: float
    power: float

class ABTestAnalyzer:
    """A/B测试分析器"""
    
    def __init__(self, alpha: float = 0.05):
        self.alpha = alpha
    
    def proportion_test(self, a_success: int, a_total: int,
                        b_success: int, b_total: int) -> ABTestResult:
        """
        比例检验（二分类数据，如偏好率）
        
        使用卡方检验
        """
        a_rate = a_success / a_total
        b_rate = b_success / b_total
        diff = b_rate - a_rate
        
        # 构建列联表
        table = [
            [a_success, a_total - a_success],
            [b_success, b_total - b_success]
        ]
        
        # 卡方检验
        chi2, p_value, dof, expected = stats.chi2_contingency(table)
        
        # 合并比例
        pooled_p = (a_success + b_success) / (a_total + b_total)
        se = np.sqrt(pooled_p * (1 - pooled_p) * (1/a_total + 1/b_total))
        
        # 95%置信区间
        z = stats.norm.ppf(1 - self.alpha / 2)
        ci_low = diff - z * se
        ci_high = diff + z * se
        
        # 效应量 (Cohen's h)
        h = 2 * (np.arcsin(np.sqrt(b_rate)) - np.arcsin(np.sqrt(a_rate)))
        
        # 统计功效 (近似)
        effect_size = abs(h)
        n = (a_total + b_total) / 2
        power = self._calculate_power_proportion(effect_size, n)
        
        return ABTestResult(
            test_type="proportion",
            a_size=a_total,
            b_size=b_total,
            a_mean=a_rate,
            b_mean=b_rate,
            difference=diff,
            p_value=p_value,
            confidence_interval=(ci_low, ci_high),
            significant=p_value < self.alpha,
            effect_size=h,
            power=power
        )
    
    def t_test(self, a_scores: np.ndarray, b_scores: np.ndarray) -> ABTestResult:
        """
        独立样本t检验（连续数据，如评分）
        """
        a_mean = np.mean(a_scores)
        b_mean = np.mean(b_scores)
        a_std = np.std(a_scores, ddof=1)
        b_std = np.std(b_scores, ddof=1)
        diff = b_mean - a_mean
        
        # Welch's t检验 (不假设方差齐性)
        t_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_ind(a_scores, b_scores, equal_var=False)
        
        # 95%置信区间
        df = len(a_scores) + len(b_scores) - 2
        se = np.sqrt(a_std**2 / len(a_scores) + b_std**2 / len(b_scores))
        t_crit = stats.t.ppf(1 - self.alpha / 2, df)
        ci_low = diff - t_crit * se
        ci_high = diff + t_crit * se
        
        # 效应量 (Cohen's d)
        pooled_std = np.sqrt(((len(a_scores)-1)*a_std**2 + (len(b_scores)-1)*b_std**2) / 
                             (len(a_scores) + len(b_scores) - 2))
        d = diff / pooled_std if pooled_std > 0 else 0
        
        # 统计功效
        power = self._calculate_power_t(abs(d), min(len(a_scores), len(b_scores)))
        
        return ABTestResult(
            test_type="t_test",
            a_size=len(a_scores),
            b_size=len(b_scores),
            a_mean=a_mean,
            b_mean=b_mean,
            difference=diff,
            p_value=p_value,
            confidence_interval=(ci_low, ci_high),
            significant=p_value < self.alpha,
            effect_size=d,
            power=power
        )
    
    def paired_t_test(self, a_scores: np.ndarray, b_scores: np.ndarray) -> ABTestResult:
        """配对t检验（同一人评估两个版本）"""
        if len(a_scores) != len(b_scores):
            raise ValueError("两组数据长度必须相同")
        
        diffs = b_scores - a_scores
        mean_diff = np.mean(diffs)
        std_diff = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)
        n = len(diffs)
        
        t_stat, p_value = stats.ttest_rel(a_scores, b_scores)
        
        df = n - 1
        se = std_diff / np.sqrt(n)
        t_crit = stats.t.ppf(1 - self.alpha / 2, df)
        ci_low = mean_diff - t_crit * se
        ci_high = mean_diff + t_crit * se
        
        # 效应量 (Cohen's d for paired)
        d = mean_diff / std_diff if std_diff > 0 else 0
        
        power = self._calculate_power_t(abs(d), n)
        
        return ABTestResult(
            test_type="paired_t_test",
            a_size=n,
            b_size=n,
            a_mean=np.mean(a_scores),
            b_mean=np.mean(b_scores),
            difference=mean_diff,
            p_value=p_value,
            confidence_interval=(ci_low, ci_high),
            significant=p_value < self.alpha,
            effect_size=d,
            power=power
        )
    
    def _calculate_power_t(self, effect_size: float, n_per_group: int) -> float:
        """计算t检验的统计功效（近似）"""
        from scipy.stats import ncf
        
        # 非中心参数
        ncp = effect_size * np.sqrt(n_per_group / 2)
        
        # 临界值
        df = 2 * n_per_group - 2
        crit = stats.t.ppf(1 - self.alpha / 2, df)
        
        # 功效 = 1 - β
        power = 1 - ncf.cdf(crit, df, ncp)
        return float(power)
    
    def _calculate_power_proportion(self, effect_size: float, n_per_group: int) -> float:
        """计算比例检验的统计功效（近似）"""
        # 使用正态近似
        z_alpha = stats.norm.ppf(1 - self.alpha / 2)
        z_beta = effect_size * np.sqrt(n_per_group / 2) - z_alpha
        power = stats.norm.cdf(z_beta)
        return float(power)
    
    def print_result(self, result: ABTestResult):
        """打印测试结果"""
        print("=" * 60)
        print("  A/B测试结果报告")
        print("=" * 60)
        print(f"测试类型: {result.test_type}")
        print()
        print(f"A组: 样本量={result.a_size}, 均值={result.a_mean:.4f}")
        print(f"B组: 样本量={result.b_size}, 均值={result.b_mean:.4f}")
        print(f"差异 (B-A): {result.difference:.4f}")
        print(f"95%置信区间: [{result.confidence_interval[0]:.4f}, {result.confidence_interval[1]:.4f}]")
        print()
        print(f"p值: {result.p_value:.6f}")
        print(f"显著性水平: α = {self.alpha}")
        print(f"统计显著: {'是 ✓' if result.significant else '否 ✗'}")
        print()
        print(f"效应量 (Cohen): {result.effect_size:.4f}")
        print(f"统计功效: {result.power:.2%}")
        print()
        
        if result.significant:
            if result.difference > 0:
                print(f"结论: B版本显著优于A版本 (p={result.p_value:.4f})")
            else:
                print(f"结论: A版本显著优于B版本 (p={result.p_value:.4f})")
        else:
            print("结论: 未检测到显著差异")
            if result.power < 0.8:
                print(f"  注意: 统计功效不足 ({result.power:.0%})，可能需要增加样本量")

# 使用示例
if __name__ == "__main__":
    analyzer = ABTestAnalyzer(alpha=0.05)
    
    print("\n=== 示例1: 比例检验（偏好率） ===")
    result1 = analyzer.proportion_test(
        a_success=35, a_total=100,  # A版本35/100人偏好
        b_success=55, b_total=100   # B版本55/100人偏好
    )
    analyzer.print_result(result1)
    
    print("\n=== 示例2: t检验（评分） ===")
    np.random.seed(42)
    a_scores = np.random.normal(4.5, 1.2, 50)
    b_scores = np.random.normal(5.2, 1.1, 50)
    result2 = analyzer.t_test(a_scores, b_scores)
    analyzer.print_result(result2)
```

---

### 9.6 A/B测试报告模板

### 9.7 案例：两种转场方案的对比测试

#### 9.7.1 测试背景

**假设**：在电子音乐风格的剪辑中，使用「故障转场」比「淡入淡出转场」更有节奏感，更受欢迎。

**变量**：
- 自变量：转场类型（A: 淡入淡出 / B: 故障转场）
- 因变量：整体偏好率、节奏感评分、流畅度评分
- 控制变量：音乐、片段、剪辑点、效果强度完全相同

#### 9.7.2 测试设计

- **测试方法**：配对比较 + 评分
- **评估者**：30人（15男15女，年龄18-35岁）
- **测试流程**：
  1. 观看介绍视频，了解测试目的
  2. 随机顺序观看A版本和B版本（中间插入10秒白屏避免记忆干扰）
  3. 对每个版本在3个维度上用7点量表打分
  4. 选择整体更喜欢哪个版本
  5. 填写简短问卷（可选）

#### 9.7.3 测试结果

| 指标 | A版本（淡入淡出） | B版本（故障转场） | 差异 | p值 | 显著 |
|------|-------------------|-------------------|------|-----|------|
| 整体偏好率 | 30% | 70% | +40% | 0.032 | 是 ✓ |
| 节奏感评分 | 4.8 | 6.2 | +1.4 | 0.001 | 是 ✓ |
| 流畅度评分 | 5.5 | 5.2 | -0.3 | 0.345 | 否 ✗ |
| 风格匹配评分 | 5.0 | 6.0 | +1.0 | 0.008 | 是 ✓ |

#### 9.7.4 结论

> [!success] 测试结论
> 故障转场在**节奏感**和**风格匹配度**上显著优于淡入淡出转场，**整体偏好率**也显著更高。
>
> **建议**：在电子音乐风格中采用故障转场作为默认转场方案。
>
> **注意**：两种转场在**流畅度**上没有显著差异，说明故障转场并没有牺牲观看流畅性。

---

## 第10章 验证集与金标准

### 10.1 什么是验证集与金标准

**验证集（Validation Set）** 是一组标准化的测试用例，用于验证系统输出的质量。每次代码修改后，在验证集上运行，确保质量不下降（回归测试）。

**金标准（Gold Standard）** 是经过专家验证、被公认为「正确答案」的参考结果。金标准是衡量系统性能的标尺。

> [!abstract] 验证集与金标准的关系
> - 金标准 = 「正确答案」（人工标注的高质量参考结果）
> - 验证集 = 「考试卷」（标准化的测试用例 + 对应的金标准）
> - 测试 = 「考试」（系统输出与金标准对比，计算得分）

**参考文档**：[[质量保证与测试体系深度研究报告]]

---

### 10.2 验证集构建方法

#### 10.2.1 验证集设计原则

| 原则 | 说明 |
|------|------|
| **代表性** | 覆盖各种常见场景和边界情况 |
| **多样性** | 不同流派、不同难度、不同长度 |
| **稳定性** | 一旦发布就不轻易变动，保证可比性 |
| **可扩展性** | 可以方便地添加新的测试用例 |
| **可复现性** | 相同输入得到相同输出 |

#### 10.2.2 验证集层级结构

建议构建**三级验证体系**：

| 层级 | 名称 | 用途 | 规模 | 运行频率 |
|------|------|------|------|----------|
| L1 | 冒烟测试集 | 快速验证基本功能正常 | 3-5个用例 | 每次代码提交 |
| L2 | 标准验证集 | 全面评估系统性能 | 20-50个用例 | 每日构建 / 每周 |
| L3 | 完整测试集 | 深度评估和版本发布 | 100+个用例 | 版本发布前 |

#### 10.2.3 验证集构建流程

```
1. 候选用例收集
   ↓
2. 质量筛选（剔除质量差的）
   ↓
3. 专家标注（生成金标准）
   ↓
4. 交叉验证（多人标注，计算一致性）
   ↓
5. 元数据标注（流派、难度、标签等）
   ↓
6. 验证集发布（版本号 + 文档）
   ↓
7. 定期更新（每季度/每年）
```

---

### 10.3 金标准案例库（10个标杆案例）

#### 10.3.1 案例库概述

精选10个标杆案例，覆盖主流流派和难度级别，作为金标准参考。每个案例包含：
- 原始音乐 + 素材片段
- 人工剪辑的参考成片（金标准）
- 详细的剪辑说明文档
- 专家评分和评语

| 编号 | 名称 | 流派 | 难度 | 时长 | 特点 |
|------|------|------|------|------|------|
| G01 | Electric Pulse | 电子 | ⭐⭐ | 2:00 | 标准4/4拍，强调节拍对齐 |
| G02 | Epic Journey | 电影 | ⭐⭐⭐ | 3:30 | 情绪递进，转场讲究 |
| G03 | Urban Flow | 嘻哈 | ⭐⭐⭐ | 2:30 | 强节奏感，歌词配合 |
| G04 | Summer Vlog | Vlog | ⭐⭐ | 3:00 | 轻松明快，日常感 |
| G05 | Midnight City | 赛博朋克 | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ | 2:45 | 故障效果，霓虹风格 |
| G06 | Romantic Piano | 抒情 | ⭐⭐ | 4:00 | 慢节奏，情绪细腻 |

#### 10.3.2 案例数据结构

```python
# gold_standard.py
"""金标准案例数据结构"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from enum import Enum

class Genre(Enum):
    ELECTRONIC = "electronic"
    CINEMATIC = "cinematic"
    HIPHOP = "hiphop"
    VLOG = "vlog"
    CYBERPUNK = "cyberpunk"
    EMOTIONAL = "emotional"
    SPORTS = "sports"
    AMBIENT = "ambient"
    RETRO = "retro"
    DOCUMENTARY = "documentary"

class Difficulty(Enum):
    EASY = 1
    MEDIUM = 2
    HARD = 3
    EXPERT = 4

@dataclass
class CutPoint:
    """剪辑点"""
    time: float  # 剪辑时间点（秒）
    clip_id: str  # 使用的片段ID
    clip_start: float  # 片段入点
    clip_end: float  # 片段出点
    transition: str = "cut"  # 转场类型
    transition_duration: float = 0.0  # 转场时长

@dataclass
class EffectKeyframe:
    """效果关键帧"""
    effect_name: str
    param_path: str
    time: float
    value: any

@dataclass
class SegmentInfo:
    """段落信息"""
    segment_type: str  # intro/verse/chorus/...
    start_time: float
    end_time: float
    description: str = ""

@dataclass
class GoldStandardCase:
    """金标准案例"""
    case_id: str
    name: str
    genre: Genre
    difficulty: Difficulty
    duration: float
    
    # 输入文件
    audio_file: str
    video_clips: List[str]  # 素材片段列表
    
    # 金标准数据
    cut_points: List[CutPoint] = field(default_factory=list)
    segments: List[SegmentInfo] = field(default_factory=list)
    effect_keyframes: List[EffectKeyframe] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # 元数据
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    description: str = ""
    expert_notes: str = ""  # 专家点评
    quality_score: float = 0.0  # 质量评分（0-10）

class GoldStandardLibrary:
    """金标准案例库"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._cases: Dict[str, GoldStandardCase] = {}
    
    def add_case(self, case: GoldStandardCase):
        self._cases[case.case_id] = case
    
    def get_case(self, case_id: str) -> Optional[GoldStandardCase]:
        return self._cases.get(case_id)
    
    def list_cases(self, genre: Genre = None, 
                   difficulty: Difficulty = None) -> List[str]:
        """列出符合条件的案例"""
        cases = list(self._cases.values())
        
        if genre:
            cases = [c for c in cases if c.genre == genre]
        if difficulty:
            cases = [c for c in cases if c.difficulty == difficulty]
        
        return [c.case_id for c in cases]
    
    def get_summary(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取所有案例的摘要信息"""
        summary = []
        for case in self._cases.values():
            summary.append({
                "case_id": case.case_id,
                "name": case.name,
                "genre": case.genre.value,
                "difficulty": case.difficulty.value,
                "duration": case.duration,
                "cut_count": len(case.cut_points),
                "quality_score": case.quality_score
            })
        return summary
    
    def evaluate_output(self, case_id: str, 
                        output_cut_points: List[CutPoint]) -> Dict:
        """
        评估系统输出与金标准的差异
        
        返回多个维度的评分
        """
        case = self.get_case(case_id)
        if not case:
            return {"error": "Case not found"}
        
        gold_times = [cp.time for cp in case.cut_points]
        output_times = [cp.time for cp in output_cut_points]
        
        # 1. 节拍对齐准确率（容差±100ms）
        tolerance = 0.1
        matched = 0
        for t in output_times:
            for gt in gold_times:
                if abs(t - gt) <= tolerance:
                    matched += 1
                    break
        
        precision = matched / len(output_times) if output_times else 0
        recall = matched / len(gold_times) if gold_times else 0
        f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0
        
        # 2. 剪辑密度差异
        gold_density = len(gold_times) / case.duration
        output_density = len(output_times) / case.duration
        density_diff = abs(output_density - gold_density) / gold_density if gold_density > 0 else 0
        density_score = max(0, 1 - density_diff)
        
        # 3. 时序相关性
        if len(gold_times) > 0 and len(output_times) > 0:
            min_len = min(len(gold_times), len(output_times))
            if min_len > 1:
                import numpy as np
                corr = np.corrcoef(
                    sorted(gold_times)[:min_len], 
                    sorted(output_times)[:min_len]
                )[0, 1]
                temporal_correlation = max(0, corr)
            else:
                temporal_correlation = 0.0
        else:
            temporal_correlation = 0.0
        
        # 4. 综合评分
        overall_score = (
            0.4 * f1 +
            0.3 * density_score +
            0.3 * temporal_correlation
        )
        
        return {
            "case_id": case_id,
            "gold_cut_count": len(gold_times),
            "output_cut_count": len(output_times),
            "matched_count": matched,
            "precision": precision,
            "recall": recall,
            "f1_score": f1,
            "density_score": density_score,
            "temporal_correlation": temporal_correlation,
            "overall_score": overall_score
        }

# 使用示例
if __name__ == "__main__":
    library = GoldStandardLibrary()
    
    # 创建一个示例案例
    sample_case = GoldStandardCase(
        case_id="G01",
        name="Electric Pulse",
        genre=Genre.ELECTRONIC,
        difficulty=Difficulty.MEDIUM,
        duration=120.0,
        audio_file="G01_audio.mp3",
        video_clips=["clip01.mp4", "clip02.mp4", "clip03.mp4"],
        description="标准电子音乐剪辑案例，强调节拍对齐",
        quality_score=8.5
    )
    
    # 添加金标准剪辑点（示例）
    sample_cuts = [
        CutPoint(0.0, "clip01", 0.0, 2.5, "cut", 0.0),
        CutPoint(2.5, "clip02", 1.0, 3.5, "cut", 0.0),
        CutPoint(5.0, "clip03", 0.5, 3.0, "cut", 0.0),
        # ... 更多剪辑点
    ]
    sample_case.cut_points = sample_cuts
    
    library.add_case(sample_case)
    
    print("金标准案例库:")
    for s in library.get_summary():
        print(f"  {s['case_id']}: {s['name']} ({s['genre']}, 难度{s['difficulty']})")
    
    # 模拟评估
    print("\n模拟评估:")
    test_output = [
        CutPoint(0.05, "clip01", 0.0, 2.5),
        CutPoint(2.48, "clip02", 1.0, 3.5),
        CutPoint(5.02, "clip03", 0.5, 3.0),
    ]
    
    result = library.evaluate_output("G01", test_output)
    for key, value in result.items():
        if isinstance(value, float):
            print(f"  {key}: {value:.3f}")
        else:
            print(f"  {key}: {value}")
```

---

### 10.4 自动评估指标

#### 10.4.1 节拍对齐指标

| 指标 | 公式/定义 | 说明 |
|------|-----------|------|
| 准确率 (Precision) | 匹配的剪辑点 / 系统输出的剪辑点 | 系统输出的剪辑点中有多少是「对的」 |
| 召回率 (Recall) | 匹配的剪辑点 / 金标准剪辑点 | 金标准的剪辑点中有多少被「找到了」 |
| F1分数 | 2 * P * R / (P + R) | 准确率和召回率的调和平均 |
| 平均绝对误差 (MAE) | 平均偏移时间 | 匹配点的平均时间偏差 |
| 均方根误差 (RMSE) | √(平均偏移²) | 对大偏差更敏感 |

#### 10.4.2 色彩一致性指标

```python
# color_metrics.py
"""色彩一致性评估指标"""

import cv2
import numpy as np
from typing import Tuple

def calculate_color_histogram(image: np.ndarray, bins: int = 32) -> np.ndarray:
    """计算颜色直方图（HSV空间）"""
    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
    
    h_hist = cv2.calcHist([hsv], [0], None, [bins], [0, 180]).flatten()
    s_hist = cv2.calcHist([hsv], [1], None, [bins], [0, 256]).flatten()
    v_hist = cv2.calcHist([hsv], [2], None, [bins], [0, 256]).flatten()
    
    # 归一化
    h_hist = h_hist / (h_hist.sum() + 1e-6)
    s_hist = s_hist / (s_hist.sum() + 1e-6)
    v_hist = v_hist / (v_hist.sum() + 1e-6)
    
    return np.concatenate([h_hist, s_hist, v_hist])

def histogram_intersection(hist1: np.ndarray, hist2: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """直方图相交（0-1，越大越相似）"""
    return float(np.sum(np.minimum(hist1, hist2)))

def chi_squared_distance(hist1: np.ndarray, hist2: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """卡方距离（越小越相似）"""
    return float(np.sum((hist1 - hist2)**2 / (hist1 + hist2 + 1e-6)))

def earth_movers_distance(hist1: np.ndarray, hist2: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """推土机距离（EMD）"""
    # 简化版：计算累积分布的差异
    cdf1 = np.cumsum(hist1)
    cdf2 = np.cumsum(hist2)
    return float(np.sum(np.abs(cdf1 - cdf2)))

def color_consistency_score(img1: np.ndarray, img2: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    综合色彩一致性评分（0-1）
    
    融合多种指标
    """
    hist1 = calculate_color_histogram(img1)
    hist2 = calculate_color_histogram(img2)
    
    intersection = histogram_intersection(hist1, hist2)
    chi2 = chi_squared_distance(hist1, hist2)
    emd = earth_movers_distance(hist1, hist2)
    
    # 归一化并融合
    chi2_norm = 1.0 / (1.0 + chi2 * 10)  # 转换为0-1，越大越好
    emd_norm = 1.0 / (1.0 + emd * 5)
    
    score = 0.5 * intersection + 0.3 * chi2_norm + 0.2 * emd_norm
    
    return float(np.clip(score, 0, 1))

# 视频级色彩一致性
def video_color_consistency(video_path: str, 
                            sample_interval: int = 30) -> Tuple[float, np.ndarray]:
    """
    计算视频整体的色彩一致性
    
    返回：平均一致性分数 + 每帧的分数序列
    """
    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(video_path)
    
    frames = []
    frame_count = 0
    
    while True:
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            break
        
        if frame_count % sample_interval == 0:
            frames.append(frame)
        
        frame_count += 1
    
    cap.release()
    
    if len(frames) < 2:
        return 1.0, np.array([1.0])
    
    # 计算相邻帧的一致性
    scores = []
    for i in range(len(frames) - 1):
        score = color_consistency_score(frames[i], frames[i+1])
        scores.append(score)
    
    scores = np.array(scores)
    avg_score = float(np.mean(scores))
    
    return avg_score, scores
```

#### 10.4.3 节奏相关性指标

```python
# rhythm_metrics.py
"""节奏相关性评估指标"""

import numpy as np
from typing import List

def cross_correlation(series1: np.ndarray, series2: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    计算两个时间序列的互相关最大值
    
    衡量两个序列的相似程度，考虑时间偏移
    """
    if len(series1) == 0 or len(series2) == 0:
        return 0.0
    
    # 归一化
    s1 = (series1 - np.mean(series1)) / (np.std(series1) + 1e-6)
    s2 = (series2 - np.mean(series2)) / (np.std(series2) + 1e-6)
    
    # 计算互相关
    correlation = np.correlate(s1, s2, mode='full')
    max_corr = np.max(correlation)
    
    # 归一化到0-1
    normalized_corr = max_corr / min(len(s1), len(s2))
    
    return float(np.clip(normalized_corr, 0, 1))

def beat_alignment_score(beat_times: np.ndarray, 
                         cut_times: np.ndarray,
                         tolerance: float = 0.1) -> Dict:
    """
    节拍对齐评分
    
    参数:
        beat_times: 节拍时间点
        cut_times: 剪辑点时间点
        tolerance: 容差（秒）
    
    返回:
        包含多个指标的字典
    """
    if len(beat_times) == 0 or len(cut_times) == 0:
        return {
            "precision": 0,
            "recall": 0,
            "f1": 0,
            "mae": float('inf'),
            "rmse": float('inf')
        }
    
    # 计算匹配
    matched_beats = set()
    matched_cuts = set()
    errors = []
    
    # 贪心匹配
    for i, cut in enumerate(cut_times):
        best_beat = -1
        best_dist = float('inf')
        
        for j, beat in enumerate(beat_times):
            if j in matched_beats:
                continue
            dist = abs(cut - beat)
            if dist < best_dist and dist <= tolerance:
                best_dist = dist
                best_beat = j
        
        if best_beat >= 0:
            matched_beats.add(best_beat)
            matched_cuts.add(i)
            errors.append(best_dist)
    
    precision = len(matched_cuts) / len(cut_times)
    recall = len(matched_beats) / len(beat_times)
    f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0
    
    mae = np.mean(errors) if errors else float('inf')
    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.array(errors)**2)) if errors else float('inf')
    
    return {
        "precision": float(precision),
        "recall": float(recall),
        "f1": float(f1),
        "mae": float(mae),
        "rmse": float(rmse),
        "matched_count": len(matched_cuts),
        "total_beats": len(beat_times),
        "total_cuts": len(cut_times)
    }

def rhythm_consistency_score(energy_curve: np.ndarray, 
                              edit_density_curve: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    节奏一致性评分
    
    衡量音乐能量曲线和剪辑密度曲线的相关程度
    """
    # 重采样到相同长度
    target_len = min(len(energy_curve), len(edit_density_curve))
    if target_len == 0:
        return 0.0
    
    e = np.interp(
        np.linspace(0, 1, target_len),
        np.linspace(0, 1, len(energy_curve)),
        energy_curve
    )
    d = np.interp(
        np.linspace(0, 1, target_len),
        np.linspace(0, 1, len(edit_density_curve)),
        edit_density_curve
    )
    
    # 计算相关系数
    corr = np.corrcoef(e, d)[0, 1]
    
    return float(np.clip(corr, 0, 1))
```

### 10.5 人工评估流程与规范

#### 10.5.1 评估者招募与培训

**评估者资质要求：**
- 有一定的视频审美能力
- 熟悉目标风格（针对风格匹配度评估）
- 认真负责，能按照规范完成评估

**培训内容：**
1. 评估目的和意义
2. 各评估维度的定义和评分标准
3. 评分量表的使用方法
4. 练习评估 + 讨论校准
5. 常见问题解答

#### 10.5.2 评估流程

```
准备阶段：
  1. 准备评估材料（视频、问卷）
  2. 随机化播放顺序
  3. 设置评估环境（安静、光线合适、显示设备校准）

评估阶段：
  1. 介绍评估目的和流程（5分钟）
  2. 练习评估（2个练习样本，10分钟）
  3. 正式评估（每个样本2-3分钟）
  4. 休息（每15-20分钟休息一次）
  5. 简短访谈（可选，收集定性反馈）

后处理阶段：
  1. 数据录入和清洗
  2. 计算评估者间一致性（信度）
  3. 统计分析
  4. 撰写报告
```

#### 10.5.3 评估者间信度计算

```python
# inter_rater_reliability.py
"""评估者间信度计算"""

import numpy as np
from scipy import stats
from typing import List

def cohens_kappa(ratings1: List[int], ratings2: List[int]) -> float:
    """
    Cohen's Kappa 系数（两个评估者，分类数据）
    
    解读:
    < 0:  一致性差
    0-0.2: 轻微一致
    0.2-0.4: 一般一致
    0.4-0.6: 中等一致
    0.6-0.8: 较高一致
    0.8-1: 几乎完全一致
    """
    n = len(ratings1)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    
    # 观测一致性
    observed_agreement = sum(1 for a, b in zip(ratings1, ratings2) if a == b) / n
    
    # 期望一致性（随机）
    all_ratings = list(set(ratings1 + ratings2))
    expected_agreement = 0
    for r in all_ratings:
        p1 = ratings1.count(r) / n
        p2 = ratings2.count(r) / n
        expected_agreement += p1 * p2
    
    if expected_agreement == 1:
        return 1.0
    
    kappa = (observed_agreement - expected_agreement) / (1 - expected_agreement)
    return float(kappa)

def intraclass_correlation(ratings: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    组内相关系数 (ICC)
    
    用于多个评估者的连续型评分数据
    
    参数:
        ratings: shape = (n_subjects, n_raters)
    """
    n_subjects, n_raters = ratings.shape
    
    if n_raters < 2:
        return 0.0
    
    # 单因素方差分析
    grand_mean = np.mean(ratings)
    subject_means = np.mean(ratings, axis=1)
    
    # 总平方和
    ss_total = np.sum((ratings - grand_mean)**2)
    
    # 被试间平方和
    ss_between = n_raters * np.sum((subject_means - grand_mean)**2)
    
    # 残差平方和
    ss_within = ss_total - ss_between
    
    # 自由度
    df_between = n_subjects - 1
    df_within = n_subjects * (n_raters - 1)
    
    # 均方
    ms_between = ss_between / df_between if df_between > 0 else 0
    ms_within = ss_within / df_within if df_within > 0 else 0
    
    # ICC(2,1) - 双向随机，单个测量
    if ms_between + (n_raters - 1) * ms_within == 0:
        return 0.0
    
    icc = (ms_between - ms_within) / (ms_between + (n_raters - 1) * ms_within)
    return float(np.clip(icc, 0, 1))

def cronbachs_alpha(ratings: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    Cronbach's Alpha 系数
    
    衡量评估量表的内部一致性
    
    参数:
        ratings: shape = (n_subjects, n_items)
    """
    n_items = ratings.shape[1]
    if n_items < 2:
        return 0.0
    
    # 各题目方差
    item_variances = np.var(ratings, axis=0, ddof=1)
    sum_item_var = np.sum(item_variances)
    
    # 总分方差
    total_scores = np.sum(ratings, axis=1)
    total_var = np.var(total_scores, ddof=1)
    
    if total_var == 0:
        return 0.0
    
    alpha = (n_items / (n_items - 1)) * (1 - sum_item_var / total_var)
    return float(np.clip(alpha, 0, 1))

# 使用示例
if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("=== Cohen's Kappa 示例 ===")
    rater1 = [1, 2, 3, 2, 1, 3, 2, 1, 3, 2]
    rater2 = [1, 2, 2, 2, 1, 3, 3, 1, 3, 2]
    kappa = cohens_kappa(rater1, rater2)
    print(f"Cohen's Kappa: {kappa:.3f}")
    
    print("\n=== ICC 示例 ===")
    np.random.seed(42)
    # 5个被试，3个评估者
    ratings = np.array([
        [5, 5, 4],
        [3, 4, 3],
        [2, 2, 3],
        [4, 5, 4],
        [1, 2, 1],
    ])
    icc = intraclass_correlation(ratings)
    print(f"ICC: {icc:.3f}")
    
    print("\n=== Cronbach's Alpha 示例 ===")
    # 10个被试，5个题目
    item_scores = np.random.randint(1, 8, (10, 5))
    alpha = cronbachs_alpha(item_scores)
    print(f"Cronbach's Alpha: {alpha:.3f}")
```

### 10.6 持续集成与回归测试

#### 10.6.1 为什么需要回归测试

每次修改代码后，你可能改进了A功能，但不小心破坏了B功能。回归测试就是确保「旧的功能没被新代码破坏」。

> [!warning] 常见回归问题
> - 优化了匹配速度，但匹配质量下降了
> - 添加了新效果，但旧效果参数映射失效了
> - 重构了代码，但某个边界条件没处理
> - 更新了依赖库，但API变化导致bug

#### 10.6.2 回归测试框架

```python
# regression_test.py
"""回归测试框架"""

import os
import json
import time
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable
from enum import Enum

class TestStatus(Enum):
    PASS = "PASS"
    FAIL = "FAIL"
    ERROR = "ERROR"
    SKIP = "SKIP"

@dataclass
class TestResult:
    """测试结果"""
    test_name: str
    status: TestStatus
    duration: float
    message: str = ""
    details: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    expected: any = None
    actual: any = None

class RegressionTestSuite:
    """回归测试套件"""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str = "regression_tests"):
        self.name = name
        self._tests: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
        self.results: List[TestResult] = []
        self.baseline_dir: str = "./baselines"
        os.makedirs(self.baseline_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    def register_test(self, name: str, test_func: Callable):
        """注册测试用例"""
        self._tests[name] = test_func
    
    def run_all(self) -> List[TestResult]:
        """运行所有测试"""
        self.results = []
        
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        print(f"  回归测试: {self.name}")
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        print(f"测试用例数: {len(self._tests)}")
        print()
        
        for name, test_func in self._tests.items():
            result = self._run_single(name, test_func)
            self.results.append(result)
            self._print_result(result)
        
        self._print_summary()
        return self.results
    
    def _run_single(self, name: str, test_func: Callable) -> TestResult:
        """运行单个测试"""
        start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            result = test_func()
            
            if result is True or result is None:
                status = TestStatus.PASS
                message = "Test passed"
            elif result is False:
                status = TestStatus.FAIL
                message = "Test failed"
            elif isinstance(result, tuple) and len(result) >= 2:
                passed = result[0]
                message = result[1] if len(result) > 1 else ""
                details = result[2] if len(result) > 2 else {}
                status = TestStatus.PASS if passed else TestStatus.FAIL
                return TestResult(
                    test_name=name,
                    status=status,
                    duration=time.time() - start_time,
                    message=message,
                    details=details if isinstance(details, dict) else {}
                )
            else:
                status = TestStatus.PASS
                message = str(result)
            
            return TestResult(
                test_name=name,
                status=status,
                duration=time.time() - start_time,
                message=message
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            return TestResult(
                test_name=name,
                status=TestStatus.ERROR,
                duration=time.time() - start_time,
                message=f"Error: {str(e)}"
            )
    
    def _print_result(self, result: TestResult):
        status_symbols = {
            TestStatus.PASS: "✓",
            TestStatus.FAIL: "✗",
            TestStatus.ERROR: "✕",
            TestStatus.SKIP: "○"
        }
        
        symbol = status_symbols.get(result.status, "?")
        status_color = {
            TestStatus.PASS: "PASS",
            TestStatus.FAIL: "FAIL",
            TestStatus.ERROR: "ERROR",
            TestStatus.SKIP: "SKIP"
        }.get(result.status, "UNKNOWN")
        
        print(f"  {symbol} [{status_color:5s}] {result.test_name}  ({result.duration:.3f}s)")
        if result.message and result.status != TestStatus.PASS:
            print(f"      {result.message}")
    
    def _print_summary(self):
        """打印汇总"""
        total = len(self.results)
        passed = sum(1 for r in self.results if r.status == TestStatus.PASS)
        failed = sum(1 for r in self.results if r.status == TestStatus.FAIL)
        errors = sum(1 for r in self.results if r.status == TestStatus.ERROR)
        total_time = sum(r.duration for r in self.results)
        
        print()
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        print(f"  测试汇总")
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        print(f"  总用例数: {total}")
        print(f"  通过: {passed}")
        print(f"  失败: {failed}")
        print(f"  错误: {errors}")
        print(f"  总耗时: {total_time:.3f}s")
        print(f"  通过率: {passed/total*100:.1f}%" if total > 0 else "  通过率: N/A")
        print()
    
    def assert_near(self, actual: float, expected: float, 
                    tolerance: float = 1e-6, name: str = "") -> bool:
        """近似相等断言"""
        diff = abs(actual - expected)
        if diff > tolerance:
            print(f"  [{name}] Expected {expected}, got {actual} (diff={diff})")
            return False
        return True
    
    def assert_array_near(self, actual: np.ndarray, expected: np.ndarray,
                          tolerance: float = 1e-6, name: str = "") -> bool:
        """数组近似相等断言"""
        if actual.shape != expected.shape:
            print(f"  [{name}] Shape mismatch: {actual.shape} vs {expected.shape}")
            return False
        
        diff = np.max(np.abs(actual - expected))
        if diff > tolerance:
            print(f"  [{name}] Max diff: {diff} (tolerance: {tolerance})")
            return False
        return True
    
    def compare_to_baseline(self, data: Dict, test_name: str,
                            update_baseline: bool = False) -> bool:
        """
        与基线数据对比
        
        如果没有基线，创建基线；否则对比
        """
        baseline_path = os.path.join(self.baseline_dir, f"{test_name}.json")
        
        if update_baseline or not os.path.exists(baseline_path):
            with open(baseline_path, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, indent=2, default=str)
            return True
        
        with open(baseline_path, 'r') as f:
            baseline = json.load(f)
        
        # 简单对比（可以根据需要扩展）
        return self._deep_compare(data, baseline, test_name)
    
    def _deep_compare(self, actual, expected, path: str = "") -> bool:
        """深度对比两个数据结构"""
        if type(actual) != type(expected):
            print(f"  [{path}] Type mismatch: {type(actual)} vs {type(expected)}")
            return False
        
        if isinstance(actual, dict):
            keys = set(actual.keys()) | set(expected.keys())
            all_ok = True
            for k in sorted(keys):
                sub_path = f"{path}.{k}" if path else k
                if k not in actual:
                    print(f"  [{sub_path}] Missing in actual")
                    all_ok = False
                elif k not in expected:
                    print(f"  [{sub_path}] Extra in actual")
                    all_ok = False
                else:
                    if not self._deep_compare(actual[k], expected[k], sub_path):
                        all_ok = False
            return all_ok
        
        elif isinstance(actual, list):
            if len(actual) != len(expected):
                print(f"  [{path}] Length mismatch: {len(actual)} vs {len(expected)}")
                return False
            all_ok = True
            for i in range(len(actual)):
                if not self._deep_compare(actual[i], expected[i], f"{path}[{i}]"):
                    all_ok = False
            return all_ok
        
        elif isinstance(actual, float):
            return self.assert_near(actual, expected, name=path)
        
        else:
            if actual != expected:
                print(f"  [{path}] Expected {expected}, got {actual}")
                return False
            return True

# 使用示例
if __name__ == "__main__":
    suite = RegressionTestSuite("音乐分析测试")
    
    # 注册测试用例
    def test_bpm_detection():
        """BPM检测测试"""
        # 模拟测试
        detected_bpm = 127.8
        expected_bpm = 128.0
        ok = abs(detected_bpm - expected_bpm) < 2.0
        return ok, f"BPM={detected_bpm:.1f}, expected={expected_bpm}"
    
    def test_beat_tracking():
        """节拍追踪测试"""
        return True, "100% 匹配"
    
    def test_band_energy():
        """频段能量测试"""
        return True
    
    suite.register_test("BPM检测", test_bpm_detection)
    suite.register_test("节拍追踪", test_beat_tracking)
    suite.register_test("频段能量", test_band_energy)
    
    # 运行所有测试
    results = suite.run_all()
```
